\sigma_{j}^{2}(t):=\dfrac{\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}_{j}^{\top}\Big(\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{*}}{n}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{X}_{i}^{\top}+\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\sum\limits_{i=\lfloor nt\rfloor+1}^{n}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{X}_{i}^{\top}\Big)\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}_{j}}{\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}_{j}^{\top}\big(\dfrac{1}{n}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{X}_{i}^{\top}\big)\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}_{j}}.

(D.67)

Under Assumptions
\mathbf{(A.1)}
-
\mathbf{(A.5)}
, we can prove that as
n,p\rightarrow\infty

\max_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}|\sigma_{j}^{2}(t)-1|=o_{p}(1).

(D.68)

Let
q^{\prime}=|\mathcal{G}|(n-2\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1)
. Combining (
D.66
) and (
D.68
), and using Lemma
C.3
, for any
t>0
, we have

{\mathbb{E}}\Big(\max_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max_{j\in\mathcal{G}}|D_{j}^{b}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)|\Big)\leq\dfrac{\log(2p^{\prime})}{t}+\dfrac{tA_{0}^{2}}{2},~\text{with}~A^{2}_{0}:=\dfrac{3}{2}.

(D.69)

Furthermore, taking
t=A_{0}^{-1}\sqrt{2\log(q^{\prime})}
in (
D.69
), we have

{\mathbb{E}}\Big(\max_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max_{j\in\mathcal{G}}|D_{j}^{b}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)|\Big)\leq A_{0}\sqrt{2\log(q^{\prime})}\big(1+\dfrac{1}{2\log q^{\prime}}\big).

(D.70)

By Theorem 5.8 in
Boucheron et al. 2013
, we have

{\mathbb{P}}\Big(\max\limits_{\tau_{0}\leq t\leq 1-\tau_{0}\atop j\in\mathcal{G}}|D_{j}^{b}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)|\geq{\mathbb{E}}\Big[\max\limits_{\tau_{0}\leq t\leq 1-\tau_{0}\atop j\in\mathcal{G}}|D_{j}^{b}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)|\Big]+z\Big|\mathcal{X}\Big)\leq\exp\Big(-\dfrac{z^{2}}{2A_{0}^{2}}\Big).

(D.71)

Based on (
D.70
), and taking
z=A_{0}\sqrt{2\log(\alpha^{-1})}
in (
D.71
), we have

c_{W_{\mathcal{G}}^{b}}(1-\alpha)\leq A_{0}\sqrt{2\log(q^{\prime})}\big(1+\dfrac{1}{2\log q^{\prime}}\big)+A_{0}\sqrt{2\log(\alpha^{-1})}.

(D.72)

After obtaining the upper bound of
c_{W_{\mathcal{G}}^{b}}(1-\alpha)
in (
D.72
), we next consider the upper bound of
\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}|C^{b,\rm II}_{j}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)|
. To this end, we define

\mathcal{E}^{\prime}=\big\{\min_{1\leq j\leq p}\widehat{\sigma}_{\epsilon}^{2}\widehat{\omega}_{j,j}\geq c_{\epsilon}\kappa_{1}^{-1}/2,~~\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\widehat{\sigma}_{\epsilon}^{2}\widehat{\omega}_{j,j}\leq 2C_{\epsilon}\kappa_{2}\big\}.

(D.73)

By Theorem
3.7
and Assumptions
\mathbf{(A.2)}
and
\mathbf{(A.3)}
, we have
{\mathbb{P}}(\mathcal{E}^{\prime})\rightarrow 1
as
n,p\rightarrow\infty
. Under
\mathcal{E}^{\prime}
, by the definition of
C^{b,\rm II}_{j}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)
in (
D.29
), we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}|C^{b,\rm II}_{j}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)|\\
\leq\underbrace{C_{1}\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{*}}{n}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t)}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}}_{\bm{\Delta}_{1}}\\
\quad\quad\quad+\underbrace{C_{1}\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{*}}{n}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(t,1)}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}}_{\bm{\Delta}_{2}},\end{array}

(D.74)

where
C_{1}:=\sqrt{C_{\epsilon}\kappa_{1}^{-1}/2}
,
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t)}
and
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(t,1)}
are defined in (
D.26
). Next, we consider
\bm{\Delta}_{1}
and
\bm{\Delta}_{2}
, respectively. Without loss of generality, we assume
\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}\in[\tau_{0},t_{0}]
.

Control of
\bm{\Delta}_{1}
.
For
\bm{\Delta}_{1}
, by the definition of
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t)}
in (
D.26
), we have

\small\bm{\Delta}_{1}\leq C_{1}\Big(\underbrace{\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}]}\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{*}}{n}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm I}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}}_{\bm{\Delta}_{1,1}}\vee\underbrace{\max\limits_{t\in[\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1-\tau_{0}]}\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{*}}{n}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}}_{\bm{\Delta}_{1,2}}\Big).

(D.75)

Control of
\bm{\Delta}_{1,1}
.
For
\bm{\Delta}_{1,1}
, consider
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm I}
in (
D.20
) with
t\in[\tau_{0},\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}]
. Conditional on
\mathcal{X}
, using concentration inequalities and by Lemma
C.8
, we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm I}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\\
\leq C\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}}\big\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{b,(0,t)}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}})}\big\|_{1}\\
\leq Cs\big(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}})}\big)\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor nt\rfloor},\\
\leq Cs^{(1)}\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}(\text{by}~\text{Lemma}~\text{\ref{lemma: esimation error bound}}).\end{array}

(D.76)

Hence, by (
D.76
), we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}\bm{\Delta}_{1,1}&=\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}]}\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{*}}{n}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm I}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty},\\
&\leq Cs\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\sqrt{n}}=o\big(\sqrt{\log(|\mathcal{G}|n)}\big),\end{array}

(D.77)

where the last equation of (
D.77
) comes from the assumption that
{s\sqrt{\log(pn)/n}=o(1)}
with
s:=s^{(1)}\vee s^{(2)}
and
|\mathcal{G}|=p^{\gamma}
for
\gamma\in(0,1]
.

Control of
\bm{\Delta}_{1,2}
.
For
\bm{\Delta}_{1,2}
, considering
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}
in (
D.24
) with
t\in[\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1-\tau_{0}]
, we have

\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\leq\|\bm{\Delta}_{1}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}+\|\bm{\Delta}_{2}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty},

(D.78)

where

\begin{array}[]{ll}\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{1}&=-\big(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{I}\big)\big(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{b,(0,t)}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}})}\big),\\
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{2}&=-\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor-\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}\rfloor}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\big(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},t)}-\mathbf{I}\big)\big(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}})}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1)}\big).\\
\end{array}

(D.79)

Hence, by (
D.79
), we need to consider
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{1}
and
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{2}
, respectively.

Control of
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{1}
.
For
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{1}
, using Lemma
C.8
for the bootstrap based samples, we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{1}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\\
\leq C\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}}\big\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{b,(0,t)}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}})}\big\|_{1},\\
\leq C\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor-\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}\rfloor}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}})}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1)}\|_{1},\\
\leq C\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}}\Big(\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}})}-\bm{\beta}^{(1)}\|_{1}+\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1)}-\bm{\beta}^{(2)}\|_{1}+\|\bm{\beta}^{(1)}-\bm{\beta}^{(2)}\|_{1}\Big).\end{array}

(D.80)

Note that we assume
\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}\in[\tau_{0},t_{0}]
. Using Lemma
C.8
, we have

\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}})}-\bm{\beta}^{(1)}\|_{1}\leq Cs\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{n\tau_{0}}},~~\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1)}-\bm{\beta}^{(2)}\|_{1}\leq C\dfrac{\lfloor nt_{0}\rfloor-\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}\rfloor}{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}\rfloor^{*}}\|\bm{\beta}^{(2)}-\bm{\beta}^{(1)}\|_{1}.

(D.81)

Combining (
D.80
) and (
D.81
), and using the fact that
\|\bm{\delta}\|_{1}\leq s\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\infty}
, we have

\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{1}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\leq C_{1}s\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}+C_{2}s\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\infty},~\text{with}~t\in[\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1-\tau_{0}].

(D.82)

Control of
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{2}
.
After bounding
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{1}
in (
D.82
), we next consider
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{2}
. Using concentration inequalities and the trianlge inequality and by Lemma
C.8
, we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{2}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\\
\leq C\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}}\big\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}})}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1)}\big\|_{1},\\
\leq C\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}}\Big(\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}})}-\bm{\beta}^{(1)}\|_{1}+\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1)}-\bm{\beta}^{(2)}\|_{1}+\|\bm{\beta}^{(1)}-\bm{\beta}^{(2)}\|_{1}\Big),\\
\leq C\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}}\Big(s\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{n\tau_{0}}}+\|\bm{\beta}^{(1)}-\bm{\beta}^{(2)}\|_{1}+\|\bm{\beta}^{(1)}-\bm{\beta}^{(2)}\|_{1}\Big)(\text{Lemma \ref{lemma: esimation error bound}}),\\
\leq C_{1}s\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}+C_{2}s\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\infty}.\end{array}

(D.83)

Combining (
D.78
), (
D.82
), and (
D.83
), by Assumption
\mathbf{(A.4)}
, we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}\bm{\Delta}_{1,2}&=\max\limits_{t\in[\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1-\tau_{0}]}\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{*}}{n}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\\
&\leq C_{1}\sqrt{n}s\dfrac{\log(pn)}{{n\tau_{0}}}+C_{2}\sqrt{n}\big(s\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{n\tau_{0}}}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\infty}\big),\\
&\leq o\big(\sqrt{\log(|\mathcal{G}|n)}\big)+C_{2}\sqrt{n}\big(s\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{n\tau_{0}}}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\infty}\big).\end{array}

(D.84)

Combining (
D.75
), (
D.77
), and (
E.50
), we have

\bm{\Delta}_{1}\leq o\big(\sqrt{\log(|\mathcal{G}|n)}\big)+C_{1}\sqrt{n}\big(s\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{n\tau_{0}}}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\infty}\big).

(D.85)

Control of
\bm{\Delta}_{2}
.
Similarly, we can obtain the upper bound for
\bm{\Delta}_{2}
as

\bm{\Delta}_{2}\leq o\big(\sqrt{\log(|\mathcal{G}|n)}\big)+C_{2}\sqrt{n}\big(s\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{n\tau_{0}}}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\infty}\big).

(D.86)

Combining (
D.74
), (
D.85
), and (
D.86
), we have

\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}|C^{b,\rm II}_{j}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)|\leq o\big(\sqrt{\log(|\mathcal{G}|n)}\big)+C_{1}\sqrt{n}\big(s\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{n\tau_{0}}}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\big).

(D.87)

Finally, using (
D.64
), (
D.72
), and (
D.87
), we obtain an upper bound of
c_{T_{\mathcal{G}}^{b}}(1-\alpha)
as

\begin{array}[]{ll}c_{T_{\mathcal{G}}^{b}}(1-\alpha)&\leq A_{0}\sqrt{2\log(q^{\prime})}\big(1+\dfrac{1}{2\log q^{\prime}}\big)+A_{0}\sqrt{2\log(\alpha^{-1})}+o\big(\sqrt{\log(|\mathcal{G}|n)}\big)\\
&\quad\quad+C_{1}\sqrt{n}\big(s\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{n\tau_{0}}}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\big).\end{array}

(D.88)

Step 2
:
In this step, we aim to prove that
{\mathbb{P}}\big(T_{\mathcal{G}}\geq c_{T_{\mathcal{G}}^{b}}(1-\alpha)\big)\rightarrow 1
as
n,p\rightarrow\infty
.
Let

\begin{array}[]{ll}c^{\rm u}_{T_{\mathcal{G}}^{b}}(1-\alpha)&=A_{0}\sqrt{2\log(q^{\prime})}\big(1+\dfrac{1}{2\log q^{\prime}}\big)+A_{0}\sqrt{2\log(\alpha^{-1})}\\
&\quad\quad\quad+o\big(\sqrt{\log(|\mathcal{G}|n)}\big)+C_{1}\sqrt{n}\big(s\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{n\tau_{0}}}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\big).\end{array}

(D.89)

Considering the upper bound obtained in (
D.88
), it is sufficient to prove
H_{1}\rightarrow 1
, where

H_{1}={\mathbb{P}}\big(T_{\mathcal{G}}\geq c^{\rm u}_{T_{\mathcal{G}}^{b}}(1-\alpha)\big).

(D.90)

By replacing
\widehat{\sigma}_{\epsilon}\widehat{\omega}_{j,j}
by its true values, we define the oracle testing statistics as

\widetilde{T}_{\mathcal{G}}=\max_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max_{j\in\mathcal{G}}\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\Big(1-\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\Big)\Big|\dfrac{\breve{\beta}^{(0,t)}_{j}-\breve{\beta}^{(t,1)}_{j}}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}}\Big|.

(D.91)

Considering (
D.90
) and (
D.91
), it is sufficient to prove
H_{2}\rightarrow 1
as
n,p\rightarrow\infty
, where

H_{2}={\mathbb{P}}\big(\widetilde{T}_{\mathcal{G}}\geq c^{\rm u}_{T_{\mathcal{G}}^{b}}(1-\alpha)+|T_{\mathcal{G}}-\widetilde{T}_{\mathcal{G}}|\big).

(D.92)

Recall
\{Z_{j}(\lfloor nt\rfloor),\tau_{0}\leq t\leq 1-\tau_{0},1\leq j\leq p\}
defined in (
D.46
). By definition, we have

\widetilde{T}_{\mathcal{G}}=\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}\dfrac{|Z_{j}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)|}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}}.

(D.93)

Let
\bm{Z}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)=\big(Z_{1}(\lfloor nt\rfloor),\ldots,Z_{p}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\big)^{\top}
. Under
\mathbf{H}_{1}
, we have the following decomposition:

\bm{Z}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)=\bm{\delta}(t)+\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{*}}{n}\Big(\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\widehat{\bm{\xi}}_{i}-\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{*}}\sum\limits_{i=\lfloor nt\rfloor+1}^{n}\widehat{\bm{\xi}}_{i}+\bm{R}^{(0,t)}-\bm{R}^{(t,1)}\Big),

(D.94)

where
\widehat{\bm{\xi}}_{i}:=(\widehat{\xi}_{i,1},\ldots,\widehat{\xi}_{i,p})^{\top}
with
\widehat{\xi}_{i,j}=\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\epsilon_{i}
,
\bm{\delta}(t)=(\delta_{1}(t),\ldots,\delta_{p}(t))^{\top}
is defined in (
D.47
),
\bm{R}^{(0,t)}
and
\bm{R}^{(t,1)}
are defined in (
D.48
).
Using (
D.94
), under the event
\mathcal{E}^{\prime}
, we have

\widetilde{T}_{\mathcal{G}}\geq\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}\dfrac{\delta_{j}(t)}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}}-(c_{\epsilon}\kappa_{2}^{-1}/2)^{-1/2}(\bm{R}_{1}+\bm{R}_{2}+\bm{R}_{3}),

(D.95)

with

\begin{array}[]{l}\bm{R}_{1}=\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{*}}{n}\Big\|\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\widehat{\bm{\xi}}_{i}-\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{*}}\sum\limits_{i=\lfloor nt\rfloor+1}^{n}\widehat{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\Big\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty},\\
\bm{R}_{2}=\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{*}}{n}\|\bm{R}^{(0,t)}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty},\\
\bm{R}_{3}=\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{*}}{n}\|\bm{R}^{(t,1)}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}.\end{array}

(D.96)

By (
D.92
) and (
D.95
), to prove
H_{2}\rightarrow 1
, it is sufficient to prove
H_{3}\rightarrow 1
, where

\begin{array}[]{ll}H_{3}&={\mathbb{P}}\Big(\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}\dfrac{\delta_{j}(t)}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}}\geq(c_{\epsilon}\kappa_{2}^{-1}/2)^{-1/2}(\bm{R}_{1}+\bm{R}_{2}+\bm{R}_{3})\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad+c^{\rm u}_{T_{\mathcal{G}}^{b}}(1-\alpha)+|T_{\mathcal{G}}-\widetilde{T}_{\mathcal{G}}|\Big).\end{array}

(D.97)

Next, we prove
H_{3}\rightarrow 1
. To this end, we need to obtain the upper bound of
\bm{R}_{1}
,
\bm{R}_{2}
, and
\bm{R}_{3}
, and
|T_{\mathcal{G}}-\widetilde{T}_{\mathcal{G}}|
, respectively.

Control of
\bm{R}_{1}
. We first consider
\bm{R}_{1}
. By Assumptions
\mathbf{(A.1)}
–
\mathbf{(A.5)}
, using basic concentration inequalities, we can prove that with probability at least
1-C_{1}(np)^{-C_{2}}
,

\bm{R}_{1}\leq C_{2}\sqrt{\log(|\mathcal{G}|n)}.

(D.98)

Control of
\bm{R}_{2}
. We next bound
\bm{R}_{2}
. Considering
\bm{R}^{(0,t)}
in (
D.48
), we have

\bm{R}_{2}\leq\underbrace{\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},t_{0}]}\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{*}}{n}\big\|\bm{R}^{(0,t),\rm I}\big\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}}_{\bm{R}_{2,1}}\vee\underbrace{\max\limits_{t\in[t_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{*}}{n}\big\|\bm{R}^{(0,t),\rm II}\big\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}}_{\bm{R}_{2,2}},

(D.99)

where
\bm{R}^{(0,t),\rm I}
and
\bm{R}^{(0,t),\rm II}
are defined in (
D.49
).
Next, we bound
\bm{R}_{2,1}
and
\bm{R}_{2,2}
, respectively.

Control of
\bm{R}_{2,1}
. For
\bm{R}_{2,1}
, using concentration inequalities and Lemma
C.8
, we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}\bm{R}_{2,1}&\leq C_{1}\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},t_{0}]}\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{*}}{n}\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}}\big\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)}-\bm{\beta}^{(1)}\big\|_{1},\\
&\leq C_{1}s\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\sqrt{n}}=o\big(\log(|\mathcal{G}|n)\big),\end{array}

(D.100)

where the last equation of (
D.100
) comes from the assumption that
s\sqrt{\log(pn)/n}=o(1)
and
|\mathcal{G}|=p^{\gamma}
with
\gamma\in(0,1]
.

Control of
\bm{R}_{2,2}
. For
\bm{R}_{2,2}
, by the decomposition in (
D.49
), we have

\bm{R}_{2,2}\leq\bm{R}_{2,2,1}+\bm{R}_{2,2,2},

(D.101)

where
\bm{R}_{2,2,1}
and
\bm{R}_{2,2,2}
are defined as

\begin{array}[]{ll}\bm{R}_{2,2,1}&=\max\limits_{t\in[t_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{*}}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt_{0}\rfloor-\lfloor nt\rfloor}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\big\|\big(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(t_{0},t)}-\mathbf{I}\big)\big(\bm{\beta}^{(1)}-\bm{\beta}^{(2)}\big)\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty},\\
&\leq C_{1}\sqrt{n}\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{n\tau_{0}}}\|\bm{\beta}^{(1)}-\bm{\beta}^{(2)}\|_{1},\\
&\leq C_{1}\sqrt{n}s\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{n\tau_{0}}}\big\|\bm{\delta}\big\|_{\infty},\end{array}

(D.102)

and

\begin{array}[]{ll}\bm{R}_{2,2,2}&=\max\limits_{t\in[t_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{*}}{n}\big\|\big(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{I}\big)\big(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)}-\bm{\beta}^{(1)}\big)\big\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty},\\
&\leq\max\limits_{t\in[t_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}C_{1}\sqrt{n}\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{n\tau_{0}}}\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)}-\bm{\beta}^{(1)}\|_{1},\\
&\leq\max\limits_{t\in[t_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}C_{1}\sqrt{n}\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{n\tau_{0}}}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor-\lfloor nt_{0}\rfloor}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\|\bm{\beta}^{(2)}-\bm{\beta}^{(1)}\|_{1},~(\text{by Lemma \ref{lemma: esimation error bound}})\\
&\leq C_{1}\sqrt{n}s\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{n\tau_{0}}}\big\|\bm{\delta}\big\|_{\infty}.\end{array}

(D.103)

Combining (
D.99
), (
D.100
), (
D.101
),
(
D.102
), (
D.103
), we have

\bm{R}_{2}\leq o\big(\log(|\mathcal{G}|n)\big)+C_{1}\sqrt{n}s\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{n\tau_{0}}}\big\|\bm{\delta}\big\|_{\infty}.

(D.104)

Control of
\bm{R}_{3}
. With a similar proof, we can obtain the upper bound of
\bm{R}_{3}
as

\bm{R}_{3}\leq o\big(\log(|\mathcal{G}|n)\big)+C_{1}\sqrt{n}s\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{n\tau_{0}}}\big\|\bm{\delta}\big\|_{\infty}.

(D.105)

Control of
|T_{\mathcal{G}}-\widetilde{T}_{\mathcal{G}}|
. After bounding
\bm{R}_{1}
,
\bm{R}_{2}
, and
\bm{R}_{3}
in (
D.98
), (
D.104
), and (
D.105
), we next bound
|T_{\mathcal{G}}-\widetilde{T}_{\mathcal{G}}|
. Using the fact that
|\max_{i}|a_{i}|-\max_{i}|b_{i}||\leq\max_{i}|a_{i}-b_{i}|
, we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}|T_{\mathcal{G}}-\widetilde{T}_{\mathcal{G}}|\\
=\Big|\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}\dfrac{|Z_{j}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)|}{\sqrt{\widehat{\sigma}_{\epsilon}^{2}\widehat{\omega}_{j,j}}}-\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}\dfrac{|Z_{j}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)|}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}}\Big|,\\
\leq\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}\Big|\dfrac{Z_{j}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)}{\sqrt{\widehat{\sigma}_{\epsilon}^{2}\widehat{\omega}_{j,j}}}-\dfrac{Z_{j}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}}\Big|,\\
\leq\widetilde{T}_{\mathcal{G}}\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}\Big|\dfrac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}}{\sqrt{\widehat{\sigma}_{\epsilon}^{2}\widehat{\omega}_{j,j}}}-1\Big|.\end{array}

(D.106)

Note that conditional on the event
\mathcal{E}^{\prime}
, using Theorem
3.7
, we have

\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}\Big|\dfrac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}}{\sqrt{\widehat{\sigma}_{\epsilon}^{2}\widehat{\omega}_{j,j}}}-1\Big|\leq C_{1}\underbrace{\max_{1\leq j\leq p}|\widehat{\sigma}_{\epsilon}^{2}\widehat{\omega}_{j,j}-\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}|}_{\epsilon_{n}^{\prime}}=o_{p}(1).

(D.107)

Considering (
D.106
) and (
D.107
), using the decomposition for
T_{\mathcal{G}}
in (
D.94
), we have

|T_{\mathcal{G}}-\widetilde{T}_{\mathcal{G}}|\leq C_{1}\epsilon_{n}^{\prime}\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}\dfrac{\delta_{j}(t)}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}}+C_{2}\epsilon_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{R}_{1}+\bm{R}_{2}+\bm{R}_{3}).

(D.108)

Let
\epsilon_{n}^{\prime\prime}=s\sqrt{\log(pn)/n\tau_{0}}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\infty}/\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}
. By the definition of
\bm{\delta}(t)
in (
D.47
), we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}\dfrac{\delta_{j}(t)}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}}&=\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt_{0}\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt_{0}\rfloor^{*}}{n}\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}\dfrac{|\beta_{j}^{(1)}-\beta_{j}^{(2)}|}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}},\\
&=\sqrt{n}\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}\Big|\dfrac{t_{0}(1-t_{0})(\beta_{j}^{(2)}-\beta_{j}^{(1)})}{(\sigma^{2}_{\epsilon}\omega_{j,j})^{1/2}}\Big|+O(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{n}}),\end{array}

(D.109)

where the last equation comes from the fact that
|\lfloor nt\rfloor/n-t|=O(1/n)
as
n\rightarrow\infty
.

Finally, for
H_{3}
in (
D.97
), considering the upper bounds in (
D.89
), (
D.98
), (
D.104
), (
D.105
), (
D.108
), we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}H_{3}&\geq{\mathbb{P}}\Big(\sqrt{n}\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}\Big|\dfrac{t_{0}(1-t_{0})(\beta_{j}^{(2)}-\beta_{j}^{(1)})}{(\sigma^{2}_{\epsilon}\omega_{j,j})^{1/2}}\Big|\geq C_{1}\sqrt{2\log(|\mathcal{G}|n)}+C_{2}\sqrt{2\log(\alpha^{-1})}\\
&\quad\quad\quad+C_{3}(\epsilon_{n}^{\prime}\vee\epsilon_{n}^{\prime\prime})(\sqrt{n}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty})\Big),\\
&\geq{\mathbb{P}}\Big(\sqrt{n}\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}\big|D_{j}\big|\geq\dfrac{C_{4}}{(1-\epsilon_{n}^{\prime}\vee\epsilon_{n}^{\prime\prime})}\big(\sqrt{2\log(|\mathcal{G}|n)}+\sqrt{2\log(\alpha^{-1})}\big)\Big).\end{array}

(D.110)

Considering (
D.110
), by choosing a large enough constant in (
3.11
), we have
H_{3}\rightarrow 1
, which completes the proof of Theorem
3.8
.

∎

Appendix E
Proofs of lemmas in Section S7

E.1
Proof of Lemma
D.1

Proof.

In this section, we aim to prove

{\mathbb{P}}\big(\max_{\tau_{0}\leq t\leq 1-\tau_{0}}\big\|\bm{C}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)-\widetilde{\bm{C}}^{\text{I}}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\big\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\geq\epsilon\big)=o(1).

(E.1)

Without loss of generality, we assume
\mathcal{G}=\{1,\ldots,p\}
. Using the triangle inequality, we have

{\mathbb{P}}\big(\max_{\tau_{0}\leq t\leq 1-\tau_{0}}\big\|\bm{C}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)-\widetilde{\bm{C}}^{\text{I}}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\big\|_{\infty}\geq\epsilon\big)\leq D_{1}+D_{2},

(E.2)

where

\begin{array}[]{l}D_{1}:={\mathbb{P}}\big(\max\limits_{\tau_{0}\leq t\leq 1-\tau_{0}}\big\|\bm{C}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)-\bm{C}^{\text{I}}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\big\|_{\infty}\geq\epsilon/2\big),\\
\\
D_{2}:={\mathbb{P}}\big(\max\limits_{\tau_{0}\leq t\leq 1-\tau_{0}}\big\|\bm{C}^{\text{I}}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)-\widetilde{\bm{C}}^{\text{I}}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\big\|_{\infty}\geq\epsilon/2\big).\end{array}

(E.3)

Control of
D_{1}
. By (
E.2
), to prove (
E.1
), we need to bound
D_{1}
and
D_{2}
, respectively. We first consider
D_{1}
. To this end, we define

\mathcal{E}=\big\{\min_{1\leq j\leq p}\widehat{\sigma}_{\epsilon}^{2}\widehat{\omega}_{j,j}>c_{\epsilon}\kappa_{1}^{-1}/2\big\},

(E.4)

where
\kappa_{2}
and
c_{\epsilon}
are defined in Assumptions
\mathbf{(A.2)}
and
\mathbf{(A.3)}
. By introuducing
\mathcal{E}
, we have

D_{1}\leq{\mathbb{P}}\big(\max\limits_{\tau_{0}\leq t\leq 1-\tau_{0}}\big\|\bm{C}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)-\bm{C}^{\text{I}}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\big\|_{\infty}\geq\epsilon/2\cap\mathcal{E}\big)+{\mathbb{P}}(\mathcal{E}^{c}).

(E.5)

By Theorem
3.3
, we have
{\mathbb{P}}(\mathcal{E}^{c})=o(1)
as
n,p\rightarrow\infty
. Under the event
\mathcal{E}
, we have

\begin{array}[]{l}{\mathbb{P}}\big(\max\limits_{\tau_{0}\leq t\leq 1-\tau_{0}}\big\|\bm{C}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)-\bm{C}^{\text{I}}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\big\|_{\infty}\geq\epsilon/2\cap\mathcal{E}\big)\\
\leq{\mathbb{P}}\big(\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{*}}{n}\big(\widehat{\sigma}_{\epsilon}^{2}\widehat{\omega}_{j,j}\big)^{-1/2}|\Delta^{(0,t)}_{j}-\Delta^{(t,1)}_{j}|\geq\epsilon/2\big),\\
\leq{\mathbb{P}}\big(\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{(0,t)}-\bm{\Delta}^{(t,1)}\|_{\infty}\geq\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{c_{\epsilon}\kappa_{2}^{-1}/2}\epsilon n^{-1/2}\big),\\
\leq{\mathbb{P}}\big(\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{(0,t)}\|_{\infty}\geq C_{1}\epsilon n^{-1/2}\big)+{\mathbb{P}}\big(\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{(t,1)}\|_{\infty}\geq C_{2}\epsilon n^{-1/2}\big).\end{array}

(E.6)

By the definitions of
\bm{\Delta}^{(0,t)}
and
\bm{\Delta}^{(t,1)}
in (
D.11
), we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}\|\bm{\Delta}^{(0,t)}\|_{\infty}&\leq\big\|\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{I}\big\|_{\infty}\big\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)}-\bm{\beta}^{(0)}\big\|_{1}.\end{array}

(E.7)

To bound
\|\bm{\Delta}^{(0,t)}\|_{\infty}
, we need to consider
\big\|\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{I}\big\|_{\infty}
and
\big\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)}-\bm{\beta}^{(0)}\big\|_{1}
, respectively. For
\big\|\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{I}\big\|_{\infty}
, by the triangle inequality, we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}\|\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{I}\big\|_{\infty}\\
\leq\|\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{n}-\mathbf{I}\big\|_{\infty}+\|(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}-\bm{\Theta})(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)})\big\|_{\infty}+\|\bm{\Theta}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)})\big\|_{\infty},\\
\leq\|\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{n}-\mathbf{I}\big\|_{\infty}+\|(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}-\bm{\Theta})(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)})\big\|_{\infty}+\|\bm{\Theta}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{n}-\mathbf{I}\big\|_{\infty}+\|\bm{\Theta}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{I}\big\|_{\infty},\\
\leq\|\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{n}-\mathbf{I}\big\|_{\infty}+\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}\|\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}_{j}-\bm{\Theta}_{j}\|_{1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)}\|_{\infty}+\|\bm{\Theta}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{n}-\mathbf{I}\big\|_{\infty}+\|\bm{\Theta}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{I}\big\|_{\infty}.\end{array}

(E.8)

To bound
\|\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{I}\big\|_{\infty}
, we consider the four parts on the RHS of (
E.8
), respectively.

For

\|\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{n}-\mathbf{I}\big\|_{\infty}
, by
Van de Geer et al. 2014
and Assumption
\mathbf{(A.5)}
, we have

\|\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{n}-\mathbf{I}\big\|_{\infty}\leq O_{p}\Big(\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}\lambda_{(j)}\Big)=O_{p}\Big(\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{n}}\Big).

(E.9)

For
\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}\|\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}_{j}-\bm{\Theta}_{j}\|_{1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)}\|_{\infty}
, by Lemma
C.4
, we have

\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\|\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}_{j}-\bm{\Theta}_{j}\|_{1}=O_{p}\Big(s_{j}\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{n}}\Big).

(E.10)

Note that we can write
\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)}\|_{\infty}
into

\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)}-\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{n}\|_{\infty}=\max_{1\leq j,k\leq p}\Big|\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\sum_{i=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\big(X_{i,j}X_{i,k}-{\mathbb{E}}(X_{i,j}X_{i,k})\big)-\dfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(X_{i,j}X_{i,k}-{\mathbb{E}}(X_{i,j}X_{i,k})\big)\Big|.

(E.11)

Under Assumption
\mathbf{(A.1)}
,
X_{i,j}X_{i,k}-{\mathbb{E}}X_{i,j}X_{i,k}
follows sub-exponential distributions for
1\leq j,k\leq p
and
1\leq i\leq n
. By Bernstein’s inequality, with probabilty tending to 1, we have

\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)}-\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{n}\|_{\infty}\leq C_{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}}.

(E.12)

For
\|\bm{\Theta}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{n}-\mathbf{I}\big\|_{\infty}+\|\bm{\Theta}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{I}\big\|_{\infty}
, using concentration inequalities again, we have

\|\bm{\Theta}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{n}-\mathbf{I}\big\|_{\infty}+\|\bm{\Theta}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{I}\big\|_{\infty}=O_{p}\Big(\sqrt{\dfrac{\log pn}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}}\Big).

(E.13)

Combining the results in (
E.9
) - (
E.13
), we have

\max_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\big\|\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{I}\big\|_{\infty}\leq O_{p}\Big(\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}}\Big).

(E.14)

Note that by Lemma
C.8
, under
\mathbf{H}_{0}
,
\sup\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\big\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)}-\bm{\beta}^{(0)}\big\|_{1}\leq s^{(0)}\sqrt{\log(p)/\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}
holds. Considering (
E.14
), we have

\max_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{(0,t)}\|_{\infty}\leq O_{p}\Big(s^{(0)}\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}\Big).

(E.15)

With a similar proof technique , for
\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{(t,1)}\|_{\infty}
, we can obtain

\max_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{(t,1)}\|_{\infty}\leq O_{p}\Big(s^{(0)}\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}\Big).

(E.16)

Note that

\epsilon=C\max(\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}s_{j}\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\sqrt{n}},s\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\log(pn)}{{\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}})

(E.17)

holds for some large enough constant
C>0
. Considering (
E.6
), (
E.15
), and (
E.16
), as
n,p\rightarrow\infty
, we have

D_{1}=o(1)
.

Control of
D_{2}
. After bounding
D_{1}
, we next consider
D_{2}
. By the definitions of
\bm{C}^{\text{I}}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)
and
\widetilde{\bm{C}}^{\text{I}}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)
in (
D.15
) and (
D.16
), and
using the triangle inequality, we have

\max_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\big\|\bm{C}^{\text{I}}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)-\widetilde{\bm{C}}^{\text{I}}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\big\|_{\infty}\leq I+II+III,

(E.18)

where
I-III
are defined as

\begin{array}[]{l}I=\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}\Big|\dfrac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}}{\sqrt{\widehat{\sigma}_{\epsilon}^{2}\widehat{\omega}_{j,j}}}-1\Big|\Big|\dfrac{\Big(\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}_{j}^{\top}-\bm{\Theta}_{j}^{\top})\mathbf{X}_{i}\epsilon_{i}-\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n}(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}_{j}^{\top}-\bm{\Theta}_{j}^{\top})\mathbf{X}_{i}\epsilon_{i}\Big)}{\sqrt{n\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}}\Big|,\\
II=\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}\Big|\dfrac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}}{\sqrt{\widehat{\sigma}_{\epsilon}^{2}\widehat{\omega}_{j,j}}}-1\Big|\Big|\dfrac{\Big(\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\bm{\Theta}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\epsilon_{i}-\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\Theta}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\epsilon_{i}\Big)}{\sqrt{n\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}}\Big|,\\
III=\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}\Big|\dfrac{\Big(\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}_{j}^{\top}-\bm{\Theta}_{j}^{\top})\mathbf{X}_{i}\epsilon_{i}-\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n}(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}_{j}^{\top}-\bm{\Theta}_{j}^{\top})\mathbf{X}_{i}\epsilon_{i}\Big)}{\sqrt{n\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}}\Big|.\\
\end{array}

(E.19)

We next consider
I
,
II
, and
III
, respectively. For
I
, we have
I\leq I^{(1)}+I^{(2)}
, where

\begin{array}[]{l}I^{(1)}=\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}\Big|\dfrac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}}{\sqrt{\widehat{\sigma}_{\epsilon}^{2}\widehat{\omega}_{j,j}}}-1\Big|,\\
I^{(2)}=\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}\Big|\dfrac{\Big(\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}_{j}^{\top}-\bm{\Theta}_{j}^{\top})\mathbf{X}_{i}\epsilon_{i}-\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n}(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}_{j}^{\top}-\bm{\Theta}_{j}^{\top})\mathbf{X}_{i}\epsilon_{i}\Big)}{\sqrt{n\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}}\Big|.\end{array}

(E.20)

To bound
I^{(1)}
, define

\begin{array}[]{ll}\widetilde{I}^{(1)}&=\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}\Big|\dfrac{\sqrt{\widehat{\sigma}_{\epsilon}^{2}\widehat{\omega}_{j,j}}}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}}-1\Big|=\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}\Big|\dfrac{\sqrt{\widehat{\sigma}_{\epsilon}^{2}\widehat{\omega}_{j,j}}-\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}}\Big|.\end{array}

(E.21)

Using the fact that
a^{2}-b^{2}=(a-b)(a+b)
, we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}\widetilde{I}^{(1)}&=\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}\Big|\dfrac{\widehat{\sigma}_{\epsilon}^{2}\widehat{\omega}_{j,j}-\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}(\sqrt{\widehat{\sigma}_{\epsilon}^{2}\widehat{\omega}_{j,j}}+\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}})}\Big|,\\
&\leq\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}\Big|\dfrac{\widehat{\sigma}_{\epsilon}^{2}\widehat{\omega}_{j,j}-\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}}\Big|,\\
&\leq C\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}\big|\widehat{\sigma}_{\epsilon}^{2}\widehat{\omega}_{j,j}-\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{j,j}\big|(\text{Assumptions}~\mathbf{(A.2)}~\text{ and}~\mathbf{(A.3)}),\\
&\leq O_{p}\Big(\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(n)}{n}}+\max_{j}\lambda_{(j)}\sqrt{s_{j}}\Big),\end{array}

(E.22)

where the last inequality comes from Theorem
3.3
. Using (
E.22
), and by Lemma C.1 in
Zhou et al. 2018
, we have

I^{(1)}\leq O_{p}\Big(\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(n)}{n}}+\max_{j}\lambda_{(j)}\sqrt{s_{j}}\Big).

(E.23)

For
I^{(2)}
, note that for two vectors
\bm{x}
and
\bm{y}
, we have
\|\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{\infty}\leq\|\bm{x}\|_{1}\|\bm{y}\|_{\infty}
. By Assumptions
\mathbf{(A.2)}
and
\mathbf{(A.3)}
, there exists a universal positive constant
C
such that

\begin{array}[]{ll}I^{(2)}&\leq C\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}\|\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}_{j}^{\top}-\bm{\Theta}_{j}^{\top}\|_{1}\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\Big\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\Big(\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbf{X}_{i}\epsilon_{i}-\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n}\mathbf{X}_{i}\epsilon_{i}\Big)\Big\|_{\infty}.\\
\end{array}

(E.24)

By Lemma
C.4
, we have
\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}\|\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}_{j}^{\top}-\bm{\Theta}_{j}^{\top}\|_{1}\leq O_{p}\Big(\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}s_{j}\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{n}}\Big)=o_{p}(1)
. Note that Assumptions
\mathbf{(A.1)}
and
\mathbf{(A.2)}
imply that
\mathbf{X}_{i}\epsilon_{i}
follows the sub-exponential distribution. Using Lemma
C.5
, we have

\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\Big\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\Big(\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbf{X}_{i}\epsilon_{i}-\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n}\mathbf{X}_{i}\epsilon_{i}\Big)\Big\|_{\infty}\leq O_{p}\big(\sqrt{\log(pn)}\big).

(E.25)

Combining (
E.23
) and (
E.24
), we have

I\leq O_{p}\Big(\max_{1\leq j\leq p}s_{j}\dfrac{\log^{3/2}(pn)}{n}+\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}\dfrac{(s_{j}\log(pn))^{3/2}}{n}\Big).

(E.26)

Similarly, for
II
and
III
, we can obtain their upper bounds as follows:

\begin{array}[]{ll}II\leq\times O_{p}\Big(\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\sqrt{n}}+\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}\dfrac{\log(pn)\sqrt{s_{j}}}{\sqrt{n}}\Big),\\
~~III\leq O_{p}\Big(\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}s_{j}\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\sqrt{n}}\Big).\end{array}

(E.27)

Considering (
E.17
), (
E.18
), (
E.19
), (
E.26
), and (
E.27
), as
n,p\rightarrow\infty
, we have
D_{2}\rightarrow 0
.

Finally, combining (
E.2
),
D_{1}\rightarrow 0
, and
D_{2}\rightarrow 0
, we complete the proof of Lemma
D.1
.
∎

E.2
Proof of Lemma
D.2

Proof.

In this section, we aim to prove

{\mathbb{P}}\big(\max_{\tau_{0}\leq t\leq 1-\tau_{0}}\big\|\bm{C}^{b}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)-\widetilde{\bm{C}}^{b,\text{I}}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\big\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\geq\epsilon|\mathcal{X}\big)=o(1).

(E.28)

Without loss of generality, we assume
\mathcal{G}=\{1,\ldots,p\}
. Using the triangle inequality, we have

{\mathbb{P}}\big(\max_{\tau_{0}\leq t\leq 1-\tau_{0}}\big\|\bm{C}^{b}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)-\widetilde{\bm{C}}^{b,\text{I}}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\big\|_{\infty}\geq\epsilon\big)\leq E_{1}+E_{2},

(E.29)

where

\begin{array}[]{l}E_{1}:={\mathbb{P}}\big(\max\limits_{\tau_{0}\leq t\leq 1-\tau_{0}}\big\|\bm{C}^{b}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)-\bm{C}^{b,\text{I}}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\big\|_{\infty}\geq\epsilon/2|\mathcal{X}\big),\\
\\
E_{2}:={\mathbb{P}}\big(\max\limits_{\tau_{0}\leq t\leq 1-\tau_{0}}\big\|\bm{C}^{b,\text{I}}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)-\widetilde{\bm{C}}^{b,\text{I}}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\big\|_{\infty}\geq\epsilon/2|\mathcal{X}\big).\end{array}

(E.30)

Hence, to prove (
E.28
), we need to prove
E_{1}\rightarrow 0
and
E_{2}\rightarrow 0
, respectively.

Control of
E_{2}
. We first consider
E_{2}
. Similar to the analysis in Section
E.1
, we can show that

\max\limits_{\tau_{0}\leq t\leq 1-\tau_{0}}\big\|\bm{C}^{b,\text{I}}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)-\widetilde{\bm{C}}^{b,\text{I}}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\big\|_{\infty}\leq O_{p}\Big(\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}s_{j}\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\sqrt{n}}\Big).

(E.31)

Note that

\epsilon:=Cs^{(0)}\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}s_{j}\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\sqrt{\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}}.

(E.32)

By choosing a large enough constant
C
in
\epsilon
, we have
E_{2}\rightarrow 0
as
n,p\rightarrow\infty
.

Control of
E_{1}
. Next, we consider
E_{1}
. Recall
\mathcal{E}
defined in (
E.4
). For
E_{1}
, we have

E_{1}\leq{\mathbb{P}}\big(\max\limits_{\tau_{0}\leq t\leq 1-\tau_{0}}\big\|\bm{C}^{b}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)-\bm{C}^{b,\text{I}}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\big\|_{\infty}\geq\epsilon/2\cap\mathcal{E}\big)+{\mathbb{P}}(\mathcal{E}^{c}).

(E.33)

By Theorem
3.3
, we have
{\mathbb{P}}(\mathcal{E}^{c})=o(1)
as
n,p\rightarrow\infty
. By the definitions of
\bm{C}^{b}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)
and
\bm{C}^{b,\rm I}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)
in (
D.31
), under the event
\mathcal{E}
, we have

\begin{array}[]{l}{\mathbb{P}}\big(\max\limits_{\tau_{0}\leq t\leq 1-\tau_{0}}\big\|\bm{C}^{b}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)-\bm{C}^{\text{I}}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\big\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\geq\epsilon/2\cap\mathcal{E}|\mathcal{X}\big)\\
\leq{\mathbb{P}}\big(\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{*}}{n}\big(\widehat{\sigma}_{\epsilon}^{2}\widehat{\omega}_{j,j}\big)^{-1/2}|\Delta^{b,\lfloor nt\rfloor}_{j}-\Delta^{b,\lfloor nt\rfloor^{*}}_{j}|\geq\epsilon/2|\mathcal{X}\big)\\
\leq{\mathbb{P}}\big(\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t)}-\bm{\Delta}^{b,(t,1)}\|_{\infty}\geq\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{c_{\epsilon}\kappa_{2}^{-1}/2}\epsilon n^{-1/2}\big)\\
\leq{\mathbb{P}}\big(\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t)}\|_{\infty}\geq C_{1}\epsilon n^{-1/2}\big)+{\mathbb{P}}\big(\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(t,1)}\|_{\infty}\geq C_{2}\epsilon n^{-1/2}\big).\end{array}

(E.34)

To bound (
E.34
), we need to obtain the upper bounds of
\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t)}\|_{\infty}
and
\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(t,1)}\|_{\infty}
.

Control of
\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t)}\|_{\infty}
. We first obtain the upper bound of
\max\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t)}\|_{\infty}
. To this end, we consider two cases:

\mathbf{Case~1:}

t\in[\tau_{0},\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}]
. In this case, by the definition of
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t)}
in (
D.26
), it reduces to

\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm I}=-\big(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{I}\big)\big(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{b,(0,t)}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}})}\big).

(E.35)

Using the fact that
\|\mathbf{A}\bm{x}\|_{\infty}\leq\|\mathbf{A}\|_{\infty}\|\bm{x}\|_{1}
, we have

\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm I}\|_{\infty}\leq\big\|\big(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{I}\big)\big\|_{\infty}\big\|\big(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{b,(0,t)}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}})}\big)\big\|_{1}.

(E.36)

By (
E.14
), we have

\max_{t\in[\tau_{0},\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}]}\big\|\big(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{I}\big)\big\|_{\infty}\leq O_{p}\Big(\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}}\Big).

(E.37)

Note that under
\mathbf{H}_{0}
, by Lemma
C.8
, the lasso estimator
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}})}
has the following properties:

\begin{array}[]{l}\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}})}-\bm{\beta}^{(0)}\|_{q}\leq O_{p}\Big((s^{(0)})^{\frac{1}{q}}\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}\rfloor}}\Big),~\text{for}~q=1,2,\\
\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1)}-\bm{\beta}^{(0)}\|_{q}\leq O_{p}\Big((s^{(0)})^{\frac{1}{q}}\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}\rfloor^{*}}}\Big),~\text{for}~q=1,2,\\
\quad\quad\text{and}~\widehat{s}^{(1)},\widehat{s}^{(2)}\leq O_{p}(s^{(0)}),\end{array}

(E.38)

where
\widehat{s}^{(1)}:=|\widehat{{\mathcal{S}}}^{(1)}|
with
\widehat{{\mathcal{S}}}^{(1)}:=\{1\leq j\leq p:\widehat{\beta}_{j}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}})}\neq 0\}
and
\widehat{s}^{(2)}=|\widehat{{\mathcal{S}}}^{(2)}|
with
\widehat{{\mathcal{S}}}^{(2)}=\{1\leq j\leq p:\widehat{\beta}_{j}^{(\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1)}\neq 0\}
. Given
\mathcal{X}
, using Lemma
C.8
again, for
q=1,2
, we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{b,(0,t)}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}})}\|_{q}&\leq O_{p}\Big(\big(\widehat{s}^{(1)}\big)^{\frac{1}{q}}\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}}\Big)\leq O_{p}\Big((s^{(0)})^{\frac{1}{q}}\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}}\Big).\\
\end{array}

(E.39)

Combining (
E.37
) and (
E.39
), conditional on
\mathcal{X}
, for the case of
t\in[\tau_{0},\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}]
, we have

\max_{t\in[\tau_{0},\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm I}\|_{\infty}\leq O_{p}\Big(s^{(0)}\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}\Big).

(E.40)

\mathbf{Case~2:}

t\in[\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1-\tau_{0}]
. In this case,
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t)}
reduces to
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}
in (
D.24
). By its definition, we can decompose
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}
into the following two terms:

\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}=\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{1}+\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{2},

(E.41)

where

\begin{array}[]{ll}\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{1}&=-\big(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{I}\big)\big(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{b,(0,t)}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}})}\big),\\
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{2}&=-\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor-\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}\rfloor}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\big(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},t)}-\mathbf{I}\big)\big(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}})}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1)}\big),\\
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},t)}&:=\dfrac{(\mathbf{X}_{(\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},t)})^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{(\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},t)}}{\lfloor nt\rfloor-\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}\rfloor+1}.\end{array}

(E.42)

By (
E.41
), for controlling
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}
, we need to consider
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{1}
and
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{2}
, respectively.

Control of
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{1}
. We first consider
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{1}
. Similar to the analysis of (
E.8
) - (
E.14
), we can prove that

\max_{t\in[\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1-\tau_{0}]}\Big\|\big(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{I}\big)\Big\|_{\infty}\leq O_{p}\Big(\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor n\widehat{\tau}_{0,\mathcal{G}}\rfloor}}\Big)\leq O_{p}\Big(\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}}\Big).

(E.43)

Note that
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{b,(0,t)}
is constructed using data both before
\lfloor n\widehat{\tau}_{0,\mathcal{G}}\rfloor
and after
\lfloor n\widehat{\tau}_{0,\mathcal{G}}\rfloor
. By Lemma
C.8
, conditional on
\mathcal{X}
, we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{b,(0,t)}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}})}\|_{1}\\
\leq C_{1}\max\Big(\widehat{s}^{(1)}\sqrt{\dfrac{\log p}{n}},\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor-\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}\rfloor}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}})}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1)}\|_{1}\Big),\\
\leq C_{2}s^{(0)}\max\Big(\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}\rfloor}},\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}\rfloor^{*}}}\Big)\big(\text{by ~(\ref{inequality: upper bounds for lasso estimation under H0}})\big),\\
\leq C_{3}s^{(0)}\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}}.\end{array}

(E.44)

Combining (
E.43
) and (
E.44
), we have

\max_{t\in[{\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1-\tau_{0}}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{1}\|_{\infty}\leq O_{p}\Big(s^{(0)}\dfrac{\log(p)}{\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}\Big).

(E.45)

Control of
\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{2}
. After bounding
\max_{t\in[{\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1-\tau_{0}}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{1}\|_{\infty}
, we next consider
\max_{t\in[{\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1-\tau_{0}}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{2}\|_{\infty}
. Similar to the analysis of (
E.8
) - (
E.14
), we have

\max_{t\in[\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1-\tau_{0}]}\Big\|\dfrac{\lfloor nt\rfloor-\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}\rfloor}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\big(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},t)}-\mathbf{I}\big)\Big\|_{\infty}\leq O_{p}\Big(\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}\rfloor}}\Big).

(E.46)

By (
E.38
), we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}})}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1)}\|_{1}\\
\leq\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}})}-\bm{\beta}^{(0)}\|_{1}+\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1)}-\bm{\beta}^{(0)}\|_{1},\\
\leq C_{1}s^{(0)}\max\Big(\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}\rfloor}},\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}}\rfloor^{*}}}\Big),\\
\leq C_{2}s^{(0)}\sqrt{\log(p)/\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}.\end{array}

(E.47)

Combining (
E.46
) and (
E.47
), we have

\max_{t\in[{\widehat{t}_{0,\mathcal{G}},1-\tau_{0}}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t),\rm II}_{2}\|_{\infty}\leq O_{p}\Big(s^{(0)}\dfrac{\log(p)}{\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}\Big).

(E.48)

Considering (
E.40
), (
E.41
), (
E.45
), and (
E.48
), we obtain

\max_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t)}\|_{\infty}\leq O_{p}\Big(s^{(0)}\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}\Big).

(E.49)

Control of
\max_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(t,1)}\|_{\infty}
. After bounding
\max_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(0,t)}\|_{\infty}
, we next consider
\max_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(t,1)}\|_{\infty}
. Using a similar proof technique, we can obtain

\max_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\|\bm{\Delta}^{b,(t,1)}\|_{\infty}\leq O_{p}\Big(s^{(0)}\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}\Big).

(E.50)

Finally, considering (
E.32
), (
E.34
), (
E.49
), and (
E.50
), by choosing a large enough constant
C
in
\epsilon
, we have
E_{1}\rightarrow 0
, which completes the proof of Lemma
D.2
.
∎

E.3
Proof of Lemma
D.3

Proof.

We give the proof by contradiction. Suppose there is a constant
c<1
such that

\delta_{j^{*}}\leq c\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}.

On one hand, by the decomposition of
\bm{Z}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)
in (
D.50
), at time point
\widehat{t}_{0}
, we have:

\begin{array}[]{ll}\|\bm{Z}(\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor)\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}&:=Z_{j^{*}}(\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor)\\
&\leq\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n{t}_{0}\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor^{*}}{n}\delta_{j^{*}}+C_{2}\sqrt{\log(|\mathcal{G}|\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor)}+o_{p}\big(\sqrt{n}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\big)\\
&\leq\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n{t}_{0}\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor^{*}}{n}c(1+o_{p}(1))\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}.\end{array}

On the other hand, at time point
{t}_{0}
, we have:

\begin{array}[]{ll}\|\bm{Z}(\lfloor n{t}_{0}\rfloor)\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}&=\max_{j\in\mathcal{G}}|Z_{j}(\lfloor n{t}_{0}\rfloor)|\\
&\geq\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n{t}_{0}\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n{t}_{0}\rfloor^{*}}{n}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}-C_{2}\sqrt{\log(|\mathcal{G}|\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor)}-o_{p}\big(\sqrt{n}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\big)\\
&\geq\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n{t}_{0}\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor^{*}}{n}(1-o_{p}(1))\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}.\end{array}

Considering the above results, we have:
{\mathbb{P}}(\|\bm{Z}(\lfloor n{t}_{0}\rfloor)\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}>\|\bm{Z}(\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor)\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty})\rightarrow 1
, which is contradicted to the fact that
\widehat{t}_{0}
is the maximizer of
\|\bm{Z}(\lfloor n{t}\rfloor)\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}
.
∎

E.4
Proof of Lemma
D.4

Proof of
\mathcal{H}_{1}
. Without loss of generality, we assume
\widehat{t}_{0}\in[t_{0},1-\tau_{0}]
. The proof proceeds in two steps. In Step 1, we prove that

\big|\max_{j\in\mathcal{G}}Z_{j}(\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor)\big|\geq\big|\min_{j\in\mathcal{G}}Z_{j}(\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor)\big|.

(E.51)

By noting that

\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}|Z_{j}(\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor)|=|\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}Z_{j}(\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor)|\vee|\min_{j\in\mathcal{G}}Z_{j}(\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor)|,

we have
\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}|Z_{j}(\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor)|=|\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}Z_{j}(\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor)\big|
. In Step 2, we prove
\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}Z_{j}(\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor)\geq 0
. Note that
Z_{j^{*}}(\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor)=\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}Z_{j}(\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor)
. Combining Steps 1 and 2, we complete the proof.

Now, we consider the two steps, respectively. By the decomposition of
\bm{Z}(\lfloor nt\rfloor)
in (
D.50
), at time point
\widehat{t}_{0}
, we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}\bm{Z}(\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor)-\bm{\delta}(\widehat{t}_{0})\\
=\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor^{*}}{n}\big(\dfrac{1}{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor}\widehat{\bm{\xi}}_{i}-\dfrac{1}{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor^{*}}\sum\limits_{i=\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\widehat{\bm{\xi}}_{i}+\bm{R}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0}),II}-\bm{R}^{(\widehat{t}_{0},1),II}\big).\end{array}

(E.52)

By Assumptions
\mathbf{(A.1)}
–
\mathbf{(A.3)}
, using concentration inequalities, we can prove that with probability at least
1-(np)^{-C_{1}}
,

\Big\|\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor^{*}}{n}\Big(\dfrac{1}{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor}\widehat{\bm{\xi}}_{i}-\dfrac{1}{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor^{*}}\sum\limits_{i=\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\widehat{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\Big)\Big\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\leq C_{2}\sqrt{\log(|\mathcal{G}|\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor)}.

(E.53)

Next, we consider the control of
\|\bm{R}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0}),II}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}
and
\|\bm{R}^{(\widehat{t}_{0},1),II}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}
.

Control of
\|\bm{R}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0}),II}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}
. By the definition of
\bm{R}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0}),II}

in (
D.49
), using the triangle inequality, we have

\big\|\bm{R}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0}),\rm II}\big\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\leq\big\|\bm{R}_{1}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0}),\rm II}\big\|_{\infty}+\big\|\bm{R}_{2}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0}),\rm II}\big\|_{\infty},

(E.54)

where
\bm{R}_{1}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0}),\rm II}
and
\bm{R}_{2}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0}),\rm II}
are defined as

\begin{array}[]{l}\bm{R}_{1}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0}),\rm II}:=-\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor-\lfloor nt_{0}\rfloor}{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor}\big(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(t_{0},\widehat{t}_{0})}-\mathbf{I}\big)\big(\bm{\beta}^{(1)}-\bm{\beta}^{(2)}\big),\\
\bm{R}_{2}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0}),\rm II}:=-\big(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,\widehat{t}_{0})}-\mathbf{I}\big)\big(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0})}-\bm{\beta}^{(1)}\big).\end{array}

(E.55)

Using the fact that
\|\mathbf{A}\bm{x}\|_{\infty}\leq\|\mathbf{A}\|_{\infty}\|\bm{x}\|_{1}
and by concentration inequalities, we have,

\begin{array}[]{ll}\|\bm{R}_{1}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0}),\rm II}\|_{\infty}&\leq C_{1}\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor}}\|\bm{\beta}^{(1)}-\bm{\beta}^{(2)}\|_{1}\leq C_{1}s\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(p)}{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor}}\|\bm{\beta}^{(1)}-\bm{\beta}^{(2)}\|_{\infty}=o(\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}),\end{array}

where the last equation comes from the assumption that
s\sqrt{\log(pn)/n\tau_{0}}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\infty}/\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}=o(1)
. For
\|\bm{R}_{2}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0}),\rm II}\|_{\infty}
, using Lemma
C.8
and concentration inequalities, we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}\|\bm{R}_{2}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0}),\rm II}\|_{\infty}&\leq C_{1}\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor}}\Big(\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor-\lfloor nt_{0}\rfloor}{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor}\big\|\bm{\beta}^{(2)}-\bm{\beta}^{(1)}\big\|_{1}\Big),\\
&\leq C_{2}\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{n\tau_{0}}}s\big\|\bm{\beta}^{(1)}-\bm{\beta}^{(2)}\big\|_{\infty}:=o(\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}).\\
\end{array}

(E.56)

Control of
\|\bm{R}^{(\widehat{t}_{0},1),II}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}
. By the definition of
\bm{R}^{(\widehat{t}_{0},1),II}
in (
D.49
), and using Lemma
C.8
, we have

\begin{array}[]{l}\|\bm{R}^{(\widehat{t}_{0},1)}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\leq\|\bm{R}^{(\widehat{t}_{0},1)}\|_{\infty}\leq O_{p}\Big(\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor^{*}}}\Big)\Big\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(\widehat{t}_{0},1)}-\bm{\beta}^{(2)}\Big\|_{1}\leq O_{p}\big(s^{(2)}\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor^{*}}\big).\end{array}

(E.57)

Note that we assume
s\sqrt{\log(pn)/n\tau_{0}}=o(1)
with
s:=s^{(1)}\vee s^{(2)}
and
|\mathcal{G}|=p^{\gamma}
with
\gamma\in(0,1]
. Considering the above results, we have

\sqrt{n}\|\bm{R}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0})}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\leq o_{p}\big(\sqrt{n}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\big),~~\sqrt{n}\|\bm{R}^{(\widehat{t}_{0},1)}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\leq o_{p}\big(\log(|\mathcal{G}|n)\big).

(E.58)

Combining (
E.52
) – (
E.58
), with probability at least
1-(np)^{-C_{1}}
, we have

\|\bm{Z}(\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor)-\bm{\delta}(\widehat{t}_{0})\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}:=\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}|Z_{j}(\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor)-\delta_{j}(\widehat{t}_{0})|\leq K^{*},

(E.59)

where
K^{*}:=C_{2}\sqrt{\log(|\mathcal{G}|n)}+o\big(\sqrt{n}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\big)
. Note that

\delta_{j}(\widehat{t}_{0}):=\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n{t}_{0}\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor^{*}}{n}(\beta_{j}^{(1)}-\beta_{j}^{(2)})\geq 0.

By (
E.59
), using the fact
|\max_{i}a_{i}-\max_{i}b_{i}|\leq\max_{i}|a_{i}-b_{i}|
for two sequences
\{a_{i}\}
and
\{b_{i}\}
, we have

\begin{array}[]{l}\min\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}Z_{j}(\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor)\geq-K^{*},\\
\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}Z_{j}(\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor)\geq\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}\delta_{j}(\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor)-K^{*}\geq K^{*},\end{array}

(E.60)

where the last inequality in (
E.60
) comes from the assumption
\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\gg\sqrt{\log(pn)/n\tau_{0}}
.
By (
E.60
), we have
|\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}Z_{j}(\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor)\big|\geq\big|\min\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}Z_{j}(\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor)|
and
\max\limits_{j\in\mathcal{G}}Z_{j}(\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor)\geq 0
, which finishes the proof of
\mathcal{H}_{1}
in Lemma
D.4
.

Proof of
\mathcal{H}_{2}
. Note that the proof of
\mathcal{H}_{2}
is similar and easier, to save space, we omit the details.

E.5
Proof of Lemma
D.5

Proof.

We first bound
\bm{R}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0}),\rm II}
. By its definition in (
D.49
), we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor^{*}}{n}\|\bm{R}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0}),\rm II}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\leq\underbrace{\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor-\lfloor nt_{0}\rfloor}{n}\big\|\big(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(t_{0},\widehat{t}_{0})}-\mathbf{I}\big)\big(\bm{\beta}^{(1)}-\bm{\beta}^{(2)}\big)\big\|_{\infty}}_{I}\\
\quad\quad+\underbrace{\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor^{*}}{n}\big\|\big(\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{(0,\widehat{t}_{0})}-\mathbf{I}\big)\big(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0})}-\bm{\beta}^{(1)}\big)\big\|_{\infty}}_{II}.\end{array}

(E.61)

For
I
, using concentration inequalities, we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}I&\leq C_{1}\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor-\lfloor nt_{0}\rfloor}{n}\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor-\lfloor nt_{0}\rfloor}}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{1},\\
&\leq C_{1}\sqrt{{\log(|\mathcal{G}|n)}\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor-\lfloor nt_{0}\rfloor}{n}}s\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\infty}.\\
\end{array}

(E.62)

For
II
, using concentration inequalities and by Lemma
C.8
, we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}II&\leq C_{2}\sqrt{n}\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor}}\big\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0})}-\bm{\beta}^{(1)}\big\|_{1}\\
&\leq C_{2}\sqrt{n}\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor}}\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor-\lfloor nt_{0}\rfloor}{n}s\big\|\bm{\delta}\big\|_{\infty},\\
&\leq C_{2}\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor-\lfloor nt_{0}\rfloor}{n}{\sqrt{\dfrac{\log(pn)}{n\tau_{0}}}s}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\infty}\\
&=o\Big(\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt_{0}\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor-\lfloor nt_{0}\rfloor}{n}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\Big).\end{array}

(E.63)

After bounding
\bm{R}^{(0,\widehat{t}_{0}),\rm II}
in (
E.62
) and (
E.63
), we next consider
\bm{R}^{(\widehat{t}_{0},1),\rm II}
. Using concentration inequalities and the upper bound of estimation error of
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(\widehat{t}_{0},1)}
for
\bm{\beta}^{(2)}
, we have

\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor^{*}}{n}\|\bm{R}^{(\widehat{t}_{0},1),\rm II}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\leq\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor n\widehat{t}_{0}\rfloor^{*}}{n}\|\bm{R}^{(\widehat{t}_{0},1),\rm II}\|_{\infty}\leq C\sqrt{n}s\dfrac{\log(|\mathcal{G}|n)}{{n}},

(E.64)

where the last inequality comes from the assumption that
|\mathcal{G}|=p^{\gamma}
with
\gamma\in(0,1]
.

We next consider
\bm{R}^{(0,t_{0}),\rm II}
and
\bm{R}^{(t_{0},1),\rm II}
. Using concentration inequalities and the upper bounds of estimation errors of
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t_{0})}
and
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(t_{0},1)}
(see Lemma
C.8
), we have

\sqrt{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt_{0}\rfloor}{n}\dfrac{\lfloor nt_{0}\rfloor^{*}}{n}\big(\|\bm{R}^{(0,t_{0}),\rm II}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\vee\|\bm{R}^{(t_{0},1),\rm II}\|_{\mathcal{G},\infty}\big)\leq C\sqrt{n}s\dfrac{\log(|\mathcal{G}|n)}{{n}}.

(E.65)

Finally, combining (
E.62
) – (
E.65
), we complete the proof.
∎

Appendix F
Proofs of useful lemmas

F.1
Proof of Lemma
C.5

Proof.

The result of Lemma
C.5
can be obtained by using Bernstein’s inequality for sub-exponential distributions. To save space, we omit the details here.
∎

F.2
Proof of Lemma
C.6

Proof.

We only consider the proof of (
C.5
). The proof of (
C.6
) is similar. Using some straightforward calculations, we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}&\dfrac{1}{2\lfloor nt\rfloor}\big\|\bm{Y}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)}\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)}\big\|_{2}^{2}-\dfrac{1}{2\lfloor nt\rfloor}\big\|\bm{Y}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)}\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)}\big\|_{2}^{2},\\
&\quad=\dfrac{1}{2\lfloor nt\rfloor}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)}(\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)})\big\|_{2}^{2}-\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)}-\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)})^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)}^{\top}(\bm{Y}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)}\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)}).\end{array}

(F.1)

By noting that
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)}
is the minimizer of
(
2.7
), we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}\dfrac{1}{2\lfloor nt\rfloor}\big\|\bm{Y}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)}\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)}\big\|_{2}^{2}-\dfrac{1}{2\lfloor nt\rfloor}\big\|\bm{Y}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)}\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)}\big\|_{2}^{2}\leq\lambda_{1}(t)\|\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)}\|_{1}-\lambda_{1}(t)\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)}\|_{1}.\end{array}

(F.2)

Note that
|\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{y}|\leq\|\bm{x}\|_{\infty}\|\bm{y}\|_{1}
for two vectors
\bm{x}
and
\bm{y}
. Combining (
F.1
) and (
F.2
), under the event
\mathcal{A}^{(1)}(t)
, taking
\lambda_{1}(t)=2\lambda^{(1)}
as defined in (
C.4
) , we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}\dfrac{1}{2\lfloor nt\rfloor}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)}(\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)})\big\|_{2}^{2}\\
\leq\lambda_{1}(t)\big\|\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)}\big\|_{1}-\lambda_{1}(t)\big\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)}\big\|_{1}+\dfrac{\lambda_{1}(t)}{2}\big\|\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)}\big\|_{1},\\
\leq\lambda_{1}(t)\big\|\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)}\big\|_{1}-\lambda_{1}(t)\big\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)}\big\|_{1}+\dfrac{\lambda_{1}(t)}{2}\big\|\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)}\big\|_{1}.\end{array}

(F.3)

Note that

|\beta^{(0,t)}_{j}|-|\widehat{\beta}^{(0,t)}_{j}|+|\beta^{(0,t)}_{j}-\widehat{\beta}^{(0,t)}_{j}|=0,~~\text{if}~~j\in J^{c}(\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)}).

(F.4)

Adding
2^{-1}\|\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)}\|_{1}
on both sides of (
F.3
), and considering (
F.4
), we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}\dfrac{1}{2\lfloor nt\rfloor}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)}(\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)})\big\|_{2}^{2}+\dfrac{\lambda_{1}(t)}{2}\big\|\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)}\big\|_{1},\\
\leq\lambda_{1}(t)\big\|\bm{\beta}^{(1)}_{J(\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)})}\big\|_{1}-\lambda_{1}(t)\big\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)}_{J(\bm{\beta}^{(1)})}\big\|_{1}+\lambda_{1}(t)\big\|(\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)})_{J(\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)})}\big\|_{1},\\
\leq 2\lambda_{1}(t)\big\|(\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)})_{J(\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)})}\big\|_{1},\end{array}

(F.5)

which completes the proof of (
C.5
).
∎

F.3
Proof of Lemma
C.7

Proof.

Note that by Lemma
C.6
, and the URE conditions in Assumption (A.3),
under the event
\{\mathcal{A}(t)\cap\mathcal{B}(t)\}
, using standard analysis of lasso estimation (see Pages 1728 – 1729 in
Bickel et al. 2009
), one can prove that (
C.8
) holds. To save space, we omit the details.
∎

F.4
Proof of Lemma
C.9

Proof.

By definitions of
\mathcal{A}(t)
and
\mathcal{B}(t)
, to prove (
C.9
), we need to bound
{\mathbb{P}}(\mathcal{A}^{c}(t))
and
{\mathbb{P}}(\mathcal{B}^{c}(t))
, respectively. We first consider
{\mathbb{P}}(\mathcal{A}^{c}(t))
. Before that, we need some notations. We denote
\bm{\beta}_{i}
as the regression coefficients for the
i
-th observation. By definition, we have

\bm{\beta}_{i}=\bm{\beta}^{(1)}\mathbf{1}\{i\leq\lfloor nt_{0}\rfloor\}+\bm{\beta}^{(2)}\mathbf{1}\{i>\lfloor nt_{0}\rfloor\}.

Recall
\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)}
as
{\bm{\beta}}^{(0,t)}=\mathop{\text{\rm arg\,min}}\limits_{\bm{\beta}\in\mathbb{R}^{p}}{\mathbb{E}}\big\|\bm{Y}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)}\bm{\beta}\big\|_{2}^{2}
. By the first order condition, we have:

{\mathbb{E}}\big[\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)}^{\top}(\bm{Y}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)}\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)})\big]={\mathbb{E}}\big[\sum_{i=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\bm{X}_{i}(Y_{i}-\bm{X}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)})\big]=\mathbf{0}.

Hence, by the first order condition, we have:

\Big\|\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\big(\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)})^{\top}(\bm{Y}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)}\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)}\big)\Big\|_{\infty}=\Big\|\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\sum_{i=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\big(\bm{X}_{i}(Y_{i}-\bm{X}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)})-{\mathbb{E}}[\bm{X}_{i}(Y_{i}-\bm{X}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)})]\big)\Big\|_{\infty}.

By noting that
Y_{i}=\bm{X}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\beta}_{i}+\epsilon_{i}
,
{\mathbb{E}}{\epsilon_{i}}=0
, and the independence between
\epsilon_{i}
and
\bm{X}_{i}
, we have:

\begin{array}[]{ll}\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\big(\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)})^{\top}(\bm{Y}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)}\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)})\\
=\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\bm{X}_{i}\epsilon_{i}+\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\big(\bm{X}_{i}\bm{X}_{i}^{\top}(\bm{\beta}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)})-{\mathbb{E}}[\bm{X}_{i}\bm{X}_{i}^{\top}(\bm{\beta}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)})]\big).\end{array}

Based on the above decomposition, we have:

\Big\|\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\big(\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)})^{\top}(\bm{Y}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)}\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)}\big)\Big\|_{\infty}\leq I+II,

where

\begin{array}[]{ll}I=\Big\|\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\bm{\epsilon}_{(0,t)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)}\Big\|_{\infty},II=\Big\|\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\big(\bm{X}_{i}\bm{X}_{i}^{\top}(\bm{\beta}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)})-{\mathbb{E}}[\bm{X}_{i}\bm{X}_{i}^{\top}(\bm{\beta}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)})]\big)\Big\|_{\infty}.\end{array}

Control of
I
. We first consider
I
. By Assumptions
\mathbf{(A.1)}
and
\mathbf{(A.2)}
, for
1\leq i\leq n
and
1\leq j\leq p
,
\epsilon_{i}X_{i,j}
follows the sub-exponential distribution. By Bernstein’s inequality, for each
x>0
, we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}{\mathbb{P}}\big(\big\|\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\bm{\epsilon}_{(0,t)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)}\big\|_{\infty}\geq x\big)\\
\quad={\mathbb{P}}\Big(\bigcup\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}\Big|\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\epsilon_{i}X_{i,j}\Big|\geq x\Big),\\
\quad\leq p\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq p}{\mathbb{P}}\Big(\Big|\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\epsilon_{i}X_{i,j}\Big|\geq x\Big),\\
\quad\leq C_{1}p\exp(-C_{2}\lfloor nt\rfloor x^{2}).\\
\end{array}

(F.6)

By (
F.6
), taking
\lambda^{(1)}=K_{1}\sqrt{\log(pn)/\lfloor nt\rfloor}
for some big enough constant
K_{1}>0
, we have

{\mathbb{P}}\big(\big\|\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\bm{\epsilon}_{(0,t)})^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)}\big\|_{\infty}\geq\lambda^{(1)}\Big)\leq C_{3}(pn)^{-C_{4}},

(F.7)

where
C_{3}
and
C_{4}
are some big enough constants.

Control of
II
. Next, we consider
II
. Note that for
t\in[\tau_{0},t_{0}]
,
II=0
. Hence, in what follows, we consider the non-trivial case that
t\in[t_{0},1-\tau_{0}]
. Let

Z_{i}=\bm{X}_{i}^{\top}(\bm{\beta}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)})/\|\bm{\beta}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)}\|_{2},~~W_{i}=\|\bm{\beta}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)}\|_{2}.

By Assumption
\mathbf{(A.1)}
,
Z_{i}
follows the sub-Gaussian distributions. Moreover, By Assumptions
\mathbf{(A.1)}
and
\mathbf{(A.2)}
, for
1\leq i\leq n
and
1\leq j\leq p
,
Z_{i}X_{i,j}
follows the sub-exponential distribution. Hence, for
II
, we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}\Big\|\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\big(\bm{X}_{i}\bm{X}_{i}^{\top}(\bm{\beta}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)})-{\mathbb{E}}[\bm{X}_{i}\bm{X}_{i}^{\top}(\bm{\beta}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)})]\big)\Big\|_{\infty}\\
\quad=\max\limits_{j}\Big|\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}W_{i}\big(X_{i,j}Z_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}[X_{i,j}Z_{i}]\big)\Big|.\end{array}

For each fixed
j
, using concentration inequality for weighted sub-exponential sums, we have:

\begin{array}[]{ll}{\mathbb{P}}(\max_{j}|\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}W_{i}\big(X_{i,j}Z_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}[X_{i,j}Z_{i}]\big)|\geq x)\\
\quad\leq p\max_{j}{\mathbb{P}}(|\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}W_{i}\big(X_{i,j}Z_{i}-{\mathbb{E}}[X_{i,j}Z_{i}]\big)|\geq x)\\
\quad\leq 2p\exp(\dfrac{-C_{1}\lfloor nt\rfloor^{2}x^{2}}{\|\bm{W}\|^{2}}).\end{array}

Note that by definition, we have
\|\bm{W}\|=\sqrt{\lfloor nt_{0}\rfloor(1-\lfloor nt_{0}\rfloor/\lfloor nt\rfloor)}\|\bm{\beta}^{(2)}-\bm{\beta}^{(1)}\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{\lfloor nt_{0}\rfloor}C_{\bm{\Delta}}
. Hence, taking
\lambda^{(1)}=K_{2}\sqrt{\log(p)}\|\bm{W}\|/\lfloor nt\rfloor=K^{\prime}_{2}\sqrt{\log(pn)/\lfloor nt\rfloor}
, we have:

{\mathbb{P}}\Big\{\Big\|\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\big(\bm{X}_{i}\bm{X}_{i}^{\top}(\bm{\beta}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)})-{\mathbb{E}}[\bm{X}_{i}\bm{X}_{i}^{\top}(\bm{\beta}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)})]\big)\Big\|_{\infty}\geq\lambda^{(1)}\Big\}\leq C_{3}(p)^{-C_{4}}.

for some big enough
C_{3},C_{4}>0
. Combining the above two cases, taking
\lambda^{(1)}=K_{1}\sqrt{\log(pn)/\lfloor nt\rfloor}
for some big enough constant
K_{1}>0
, we have:

{\mathbb{P}}\Big\{\big\|\dfrac{1}{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\big(\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)})^{\top}(\bm{Y}_{(0,t)}-\mathbf{X}_{(0,t)}\bm{\beta}^{(0,t)}\big\|_{\infty}\geq\lambda^{(1)}\Big\}\leq C_{3}(pn)^{-C_{4}}.

With similar arguments as above, we can also prove that

{\mathbb{P}}\Big(\mathcal{B}^{c}(t)\Big)\leq C_{3}(np)^{-C_{4}}.

(F.8)

Finally, combining (
F.7
) and (
F.8
),
and noting that

\begin{array}[]{ll}{\mathbb{P}}\big(\bigcap\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\big\{\mathcal{A}(t)\cap\mathcal{B}(t)\big\}\\
\quad=1-{\mathbb{P}}\big(\bigcup\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\big\{\mathcal{A}^{c}(t)\cup\mathcal{B}^{c}(t)\big\},\\
\quad\geq 1-\sum\limits_{t\in[\tau_{0},1-\tau_{0}]}\big({\mathbb{P}}(\mathcal{A}^{c}(t))+{\mathbb{P}}(\mathcal{B}^{c}(t))\big),\\
\quad\geq 1-C_{1}(np)^{-C_{2}},\end{array}

(F.9)

we complete the proof of Lemma
C.9
.
∎
